x + 1] + 1.414); if (i > 0 && j < gz - 1) v = Math.min(v, D[q + gx - 1] + 1.414); if (i === gx - 1 || j === gz - 1) v = Math.min(v, 1); D[q] = v;
        const x = L.b.x0 + (i + .5) * K * L.step, z = L.b.z0 + (j + .5) * K * L.step, dc = Math.hypot(x - CITY.x, z - CITY.z);
        let nearV = false; for (const vv of VILLAGES) if (!vv.city && Math.hypot(x - vv.x, z - vv.z) < 260) { nearV = true; break; }
        const sc = v - (dc < 700 ? 6 : 0) - (nearV ? 3 : 0); if (sc > best) { best = sc; bq = q; } }
      out.push({ k, name: RNAME[k], x: L.b.x0 + ((bq % gx) + .5) * K * L.step, z: L.b.z0 + (((bq / gx) | 0) + .5) * K * L.step, area: area[k] });
    }
    M.reg = out;
    const t = L.b.tight, mx = (t.x0 + t.x1) / 2, mz = (t.z0 + t.z1) / 2, sea = [];
    for (const [ux, uz] of [[0.25, -1], [-0.3, 1], [-1, 0.2], [1, -0.2]]) for (let r = 200; r < 6000; r += 40) { const x = mx + ux * r, z = mz + uz * r; if (LIB.coastDist(x, z) < -240) { sea.push({ x, z }); break; } }
    M.seaLab = sea;
  }
  // البلاطات التفصيلية (L1): ٦٤٠م لكل بلاطة بدقة ٢٫٥م/بكسل، تُولَّد عند الحاجة وتُحفظ (الأقدم استعمالاً يُحذف)
  const TS = 640, TN = 256;
  function tileReq(tx, tz) {
    const k = tx + ',' + tz; let t = M.tiles.get(k);
    if (!t) { t = { tx, tz, J: null, cv: null, ready: false, used: 0 }; M.tiles.set(k, t); }
    t.used = now(); if (!t.ready && M.want.indexOf(t) < 0) M.want.push(t); return t;
  }
  function tilesTrim() {
    if (M.tiles.size <= 28) return;
    const a = [...M.tiles.values()].sort((p, q) => p.used - q.used);
    for (let i = 0; i < a.length - 28; i++) { M.tiles.delete(a[i].tx + ',' + a[i].tz); const w = M.want.indexOf(a[i]); if (w >= 0) M.want.splice(w, 1); }
  }
  function tileRun(t, tEnd) {
    if (!t.J) t.J = terrNew(t.tx * TS, t.tz * TS, TS / TN, TN, TN, 2.4, 20);
    if (terrRun(t.J, tEnd)) { t.cv = toCanvas(t.J); t.J = null; t.ready = true; const w = M.want.indexOf(t); if (w >= 0) M.want.splice(w, 1); bump(); }
  }
  // يرسم لوحة عالمية (x0,z0 لكل بكسل step) داخل منظور P مع قص المصدر على الجزء الظاهر فقط
  function blitWorld(g, P, cv, x0, z0, step, nx, nz) {
    const vx0 = Math.max(x0, P.x0), vx1 = Math.min(x0 + nx * step, P.x1), vz0 = Math.max(z0, P.z0), vz1 = Math.min(z0 + nz * step, P.z1);
    if (vx1 <= vx0 || vz1 <= vz0) return;
    const sx = Math.max(0, Math.floor((vx0 - x0) / step) - 1), sz = Math.max(0, Math.floor((vz0 - z0) / step) - 1), ex = Math.min(nx, Math.ceil((vx1 - x0) / step) + 1), ez = Math.min(nz, Math.ceil((vz1 - z0) / step) + 1);
    g.drawImage(cv, sx, sz, ex - sx, ez - sz, P.X(x0 + sx * step), P.Y(z0 + sz * step), (ex - sx) * step * P.s, (ez - sz) * step * P.s);
  }
  function drawTerrain(g, P) {
    g.fillStyle = SEA_FILL; g.fillRect(0, 0, P.W, P.H);
    g.imageSmoothingEnabled = true; g.imageSmoothingQuality = 'high';
    const L = M.L0;
    if (L && L.ready) blitWorld(g, P, L.cv, L.b.x0, L.b.z0, L.step, L.nx, L.nz);
    else if (P.quick) g.drawImage(P.quick.cv, P.X(P.quick.x0), P.Y(P.quick.z0), P.quick.w * P.s, P.quick.w * P.s);
    if (P.s > 0.22 && (!L || !L.ready || L.step * P.s > 1.6)) {     // تفصيل أدق عند التكبير
      const t0x = Math.floor(P.x0 / TS), t1x = Math.floor(P.x1 / TS), t0z = Math.floor(P.z0 / TS), t1z = Math.floor(P.z1 / TS);
      if ((t1x - t0x + 1) * (t1z - t0z + 1) <= 12)
        for (let tz = t0z; tz <= t1z; tz++) for (let tx = t0x; tx <= t1x; tx++) { const t = tileReq(tx, tz); if (t.ready) blitWorld(g, P, t.cv, tx * TS, tz * TS, TS / TN, TN, TN); }
    }
    const F = M.forest; if (F) { g.globalAlpha = P.s > 0.5 ? 0.45 : P.s > 0.25 ? 0.7 : 1; blitWorld(g, P, F.cv, F.x0, F.z0, F.step, F.nx, F.nz); g.globalAlpha = 1; }
  }

  /* ── (٢) الأشجار: القديمة (شبكة ١٤م، نفس قواعد treeCandidate) + طبيعة v19 (خلايا ٦٤م من V19.NS.nat.cells) ── */
  // الفئات: 0 قيقب، 1 صنوبر (v34: الأنواع القديمة حُذفت)
  const NKIND = { maple33: 0, willow33: 0, oak33: 0, pine33: 1 };   // v34: صنوبر v4 وقيقب وصفصاف فقط
  const CROWN = [3.6, 4.2, 1.5, 3.0, 2.6];
  function oldTree(ix, iz) {                  // نسخة treeCandidate بلا فحص الصخور (لا تلمس ذاكرة الصخور)، رفض الكثافة أولاً لأنه الأرخص
    const x = (ix + .5) * 14 + (treeHash(ix, iz, 17) - .5) * 4, z = (iz + .5) * 14 + (treeHash(ix, iz, 31) - .5) * 4;
    if (Math.abs(x) > CFG.HW - 100 || Math.abs(z) > CFG.HH - 100) return null;
    const hd = treeHash(ix, iz, 49); if (hd > 0.34) return null;
    const w = LIB.regionW(x, z); let region = 1; for (let i = 2; i < 6; i++) if (w[i] > w[region]) region = i;
    if (!(region === 1 || region === 2 || region === 4) || w[region] < .92) return null;
    const nat = window.__NAT19 !== false, density = (region === 1 ? .34 : region === 4 ? .025 : .0015) * (nat && region === 1 && window.__QUALITY !== 'high' ? 0.5 : 1), quality = Math.min(1, Math.max(.35, S.veg));
    if (hd > density * quality) return null;
    const h = LIB.height(x, z); if (h < 2.6 || LIB.coastDist(x, z) < 80) return null;
    const mt = LIB.mtn(x, z); if (mt > 0 && mt > 70 + 45 * treeHash(ix, iz, 53)) return null;
    const bank = treeBank(region); if (!bank) return null;
    if (nat) for (const v of VILLAGES) if (!v.city && Math.abs(x - v.x) < 300 && Math.abs(z - v.z) < 300 && Math.hypot(x - v.x, z - v.z) < 300) return null;
    const model = T33.pick(x, z, treeHash(ix, iz, 71)); if (!model) return null;
    const r = model.radius * (.86 + treeHash(ix, iz, 89) * .22); if (treeExcluded(x, z, r)) return null;
    return { x, z, c: model.kind === 'pine' ? 1 : 0, s: 1 };
  }
  const GC = 40, BK = 160;
  function tdInit() {
    const b = M.L0.b.tight, gx = Math.ceil((b.x1 - b.x0) / GC), gz = Math.ceil((b.z1 - b.z0) / GC);
    M.TD = { b, gx, gz, dens: new Float32Array(gx * gz * 4), old: { i0: Math.floor(b.x0 / 14), i1: Math.floor(b.x1 / 14), j: Math.floor(b.z0 / 14), j1: Math.floor(b.z1 / 14), done: false },
      nat: { cells: null, k: 0, done: false }, bk: new Map(), n: 0, nNat: 0, fDirty: false, fT: 0 };
  }
  function tdAdd(t, wgt, src) {
    const D = M.TD, i = Math.floor((t.x - D.b.x0) / GC), j = Math.floor((t.z - D.b.z0) / GC);
    if (t.c < 4 && i >= 0 && j >= 0 && i < D.gx && j < D.gz) D.dens[(j * D.gx + i) * 4 + t.c] += wgt;
    const k = Math.floor(t.x / BK) + ',' + Math.floor(t.z / BK); let a = D.bk.get(k); if (!a) D.bk.set(k, a = []);
    a.push({ x: t.x, z: t.z, c: t.c, s: t.s || 1, src }); D.n++; D.fDirty = true;
  }
  function tdOld(tEnd) {
    const O = M.TD.old;
    while (O.j <= O.j1) { for (let i = O.i0; i <= O.i1; i++) { const t = oldTree(i, O.j); if (t) tdAdd(t, 1, 0); } O.j++; if (now() > tEnd) return false; }
    O.done = true; return true;
  }
  // طبيعة v19: كل الخلايا قرب القرى والمدينة كاملة، والبرية خلية من كل أربع (وزن ٤). تعمل والخريطة مفتوحة فقط (اللعبة متوقفة) كي لا تزاحم ذاكرة خلايا الطبيعة أثناء اللعب
  function tdNatCells() {
    const b = M.TD.b, out = [];
    for (let cz = Math.floor(b.z0 / 64); cz <= Math.floor(b.z1 / 64); cz++) for (let cx = Math.floor(b.x0 / 64); cx <= Math.floor(b.x1 / 64); cx++) {
      const x = (cx + .5) * 64, z = (cz + .5) * 64; if (LIB.coastDist(x, z) < -30) continue;
      let full = Math.hypot(x - CITY.x, z - CITY.z) < 640; if (!full) for (const v of VILLAGES) if (!v.city && Math.abs(x - v.x) < 290 && Math.abs(z - v.z) < 290) { full = true; break; }
      if (full) out.push([cx, cz, 1]); else if (!(cx & 1) && !(cz & 1)) out.push([cx, cz, 4]);
    }
    return out;
  }
  function tdNat(tEnd) {
    const N = M.TD.nat, nat = window.V19 && V19.NS && V19.NS.nat; if (!nat || !nat.cells) return false;
    if (!N.cells) N.cells = tdNatCells();
    while (N.k < N.cells.length) {
      const [cx, cz, wgt] = N.cells[N.k++], c = nat.cells(cx, cz);
      for (const t of (c.t33 || [])) { const k = NKIND[t.kind]; if (k === undefined || t.court) continue; tdAdd({ x: t.x, z: t.z, c: k, s: t.s }, wgt, wgt > 1 ? 2 : 1); M.TD.nNat++; }
      if (now() > tEnd) return false;
    }
    N.done = true; return true;
  }
  function forestBuild() {
    const D = M.TD; D.fDirty = false; D.fT = now();
    let cv = M.forest && M.forest.cv; if (!cv) { cv = document.createElement('canvas'); cv.width = D.gx; cv.height = D.gz; }
    const g = cv.getContext('2d'), img = g.createImageData(D.gx, D.gz), d = img.data;
    for (let q = 0; q < D.gx * D.gz; q++) {
      const a = D.dens[q * 4], p = D.dens[q * 4 + 1], c = D.dens[q * 4 + 2], m = D.dens[q * 4 + 3], tot = a + p + c + m; if (tot < 0.2) continue;
      const pw = m / tot, cw = (p + c) / tot, o = q * 4;
      d[o] = lerp(lerp(82, 58, cw), 132, pw); d[o + 1] = lerp(lerp(108, 92, cw), 146, pw); d[o + 2] = lerp(lerp(52, 60, cw), 64, pw); d[o + 3] = 255 * 0.62 * sst(0.25, 4.5, tot);
    }
    g.putImageData(img, 0, 0); M.forest = { cv, x0: D.b.x0, z0: D.b.z0, step: GC, nx: D.gx, nz: D.gz }; bump();
  }
  // أشجار منطقة محددة (مصغّرة مكبّرة أو خريطة مكبّرة): دقيقة من المصادر نفسها
  function treesExact(x0, z0, x1, z1) {
    const out = [];
    for (let j = Math.floor(z0 / 14); j <= Math.floor(z1 / 14); j++) for (let i = Math.floor(x0 / 14); i <= Math.floor(x1 / 14); i++) { const t = oldTree(i, j); if (t) out.push(t); }
    const nat = window.V19 && V19.NS && V19.NS.nat;
    if (nat && nat.cells) for (let cz = Math.floor(z0 / 64); cz <= Math.floor(z1 / 64); cz++) for (let cx = Math.floor(x0 / 64); cx <= Math.floor(x1 / 64); cx++)
      for (const t of (nat.cells(cx, cz).t33 || [])) { const k = NKIND[t.kind]; if (k === undefined) continue; if (t.x >= x0 && t.x <= x1 && t.z >= z0 && t.z <= z1) out.push({ x: t.x, z: t.z, c: k, s: t.s }); }
    return out;
  }
  function treesIndexed(P) {
    const D = M.TD, out = []; if (!D) return out;
    for (let bz = Math.floor(P.z0 / BK); bz <= Math.floor(P.z1 / BK); bz++) for (let bx = Math.floor(P.x0 / BK); bx <= Math.floor(P.x1 / BK); bx++) { const a = D.bk.get(bx + ',' + bz); if (a) for (const t of a) out.push(t); }
    return out;
  }
  // رموز الأشجار: لوحات صغيرة جاهزة لكل فئة وحجم (لا خطوط متقاطعة في أي رمز)
  function sprites(dpr) {
    if (M.sprites && M.spDpr === dpr) return M.sprites;
    const SZ = [3.4, 4.6, 6.2, 8.4, 11.5], all = [];
    for (let c = 0; c < 5; c++) { all[c] = SZ.map(z => { const r = z * dpr, pad = Math.ceil(r * 0.6 + 2), cv = document.createElement('canvas'); cv.width = cv.height = Math.ceil(2 * r + 2 * pad); const g = cv.getContext('2d'); g.translate(cv.width / 2, cv.height / 2); treeIcon(g, c, r); return { cv, o: cv.width / 2, r }; }); }
    M.sprites = all; M.spDpr = dpr; return all;
  }
  function treeIcon(g, c, r) {
    g.lineJoin = 'round'; g.lineCap = 'round';
    if (c === 0) {
      g.fillStyle = 'rgba(46,34,14,.28)'; g.beginPath(); g.ellipse(r * 0.28, r * 0.32, r * 0.92, r * 0.78, 0, 0, TAU); g.fill();
      const gr = g.createRadialGradient(-r * 0.35, -r * 0.4, r * 0.1, 0, 0, r * 1.05); gr.addColorStop(0, '#b7c27c'); gr.addColorStop(0.55, '#7e9550'); gr.addColorStop(1, '#55693a');
      g.fillStyle = gr; g.beginPath(); for (let k = 0; k < 7; k++) { const a = k / 7 * TAU, rr = r * (0.86 + 0.14 * ((k * 5) % 3) / 2); g.lineTo(Math.cos(a) * rr, Math.sin(a) * rr); } g.closePath(); g.fill();
      g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(42,52,24,.85)'; g.lineWidth = Math.max(0.8, r * 0.14); g.stroke();
    } else if (c === 1) {
      g.fillStyle = 'rgba(46,34,14,.28)'; g.beginPath(); g.ellipse(r * 0.3, r * 0.75, r * 0.75, r * 0.32, 0, 0, TAU); g.fill();
      const gr = g.createLinearGradient(-r, 0, r, 0); gr.addColorStop(0, '#7f9a62'); gr.addColorStop(1, '#3f5a3c');
      g.fillStyle = gr; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(0, -r * 1.05); g.quadraticCurveTo(r * 0.45, -r * 0.2, r * 0.8, r * 0.72); g.quadraticCurveTo(0, r * 0.95, -r * 0.8, r * 0.72); g.quadraticCurveTo(-r * 0.45, -r * 0.2, 0, -r * 1.05); g.closePath(); g.fill();
      g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(30,44,28,.9)'; g.lineWidth = Math.max(0.8, r * 0.13); g.stroke();
    } else if (c === 2) {
      g.fillStyle = 'rgba(46,34,14,.28)'; g.beginPath(); g.ellipse(r * 0.22, r * 0.95, r * 0.42, r * 0.2, 0, 0, TAU); g.fill();
      const gr = g.createLinearGradient(-r * 0.4, 0, r * 0.4, 0); gr.addColorStop(0, '#5f8459'); gr.addColorStop(1, '#2c4a35');
      g.fillStyle = gr; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(0, -r * 1.15); g.quadraticCurveTo(r * 0.5, -r * 0.35, r * 0.36, r * 0.75); g.quadraticCurveTo(0, r * 1.05, -r * 0.36, r * 0.75); g.quadraticCurveTo(-r * 0.5, -r * 0.35, 0, -r * 1.15); g.closePath(); g.fill();
      g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(22,38,26,.9)'; g.lineWidth = Math.max(0.7, r * 0.12); g.stroke();
    } else if (c === 3) {
      const tx = r * 0.08, ty = -r * 0.25;
      g.strokeStyle = '#7a5a34'; g.lineWidth = Math.max(1, r * 0.2); g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(-r * 0.05, r * 0.95); g.quadraticCurveTo(r * 0.12, r * 0.35, tx, ty); g.stroke();
      g.strokeStyle = '#6f8f3e'; g.lineWidth = Math.max(1, r * 0.24);
      for (const a of [-168, -128, -92, -54, -14, 22, 158]) { const ar = a * D2R, ex = tx + Math.cos(ar) * r * 0.95, ey = ty + Math.sin(ar) * r * 0.75 + r * 0.32; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(tx, ty); g.quadraticCurveTo(tx + Math.cos(ar) * r * 0.55, ty + Math.sin(ar) * r * 0.55 - r * 0.25, ex, ey); g.stroke(); }
    } else {
      g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(96,72,44,.9)'; g.lineWidth = Math.max(0.8, r * 0.16); g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(0, r * 0.9); g.lineTo(0, -r * 0.2); g.quadraticCurveTo(r * 0.1, -r * 0.6, r * 0.55, -r * 0.8); g.moveTo(0, -r * 0.05); g.quadraticCurveTo(-r * 0.2, -r * 0.45, -r * 0.6, -r * 0.6); g.stroke();
    }
  }
  function drawTrees(g, P, list) {
    if (!list.length) return; const sp = sprites(P.dpr), pad = 12 * P.dpr;
    const vis = []; for (const t of list) { const x = P.X(t.x), y = P.Y(t.z); if (x < -pad || y < -pad || x > P.W + pad || y > P.H + pad) continue; vis.push([y, x, t]); }
    vis.sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0]);
    for (const [y, x, t] of vis) {
      const want = cl(CROWN[t.c] * (t.s || 1) * 1.15 * P.s, 3.2 * P.dpr, 11.5 * P.dpr), set = sp[t.c]; let b = set[0];
      for (const q of set) if (Math.abs(q.r - want) < Math.abs(b.r - want)) b = q;
      g.drawImage(b.cv, x - b.o, y - b.o);
    }
  }

  /* ── (٣) الطبقات المرسومة: الطرق، المدينة وأحياؤها، القرى، الإسطبل، الأسماء ── */
  function poly(g, P, pts, flat) {
    g.beginPath();
    if (flat) for (let i = 0; i < pts.length; i += 2) { const x = P.X(pts[i]), y = P.Y(pts[i + 1]); if (i) g.lineTo(x, y); else g.moveTo(x, y); }
    else for (let i = 0; i < pts.length; i++) { const x = P.X(pts[i][0]), y = P.Y(pts[i][1]); if (i) g.lineTo(x, y); else g.moveTo(x, y); }
  }
  function drawRoads(g, P) {
    g.lineJoin = 'round'; g.lineCap = 'round';
    const wc = Math.max(7.5 * P.s, 2.6 * P.dpr), wf = Math.max(5.2 * P.s, 1.3 * P.dpr);
    for (const pts of WORLD.roads) { poly(g, P, pts, true); g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(92,64,34,.88)'; g.lineWidth = wc; g.stroke(); }
    for (const pts of WORLD.roads) { poly(g, P, pts, true); g.strokeStyle = '#f4e7c5'; g.lineWidth = wf; g.stroke(); }
    if (P.s > 0.9) for (const pts of WORLD.roads) { poly(g, P, pts, true); g.setLineDash([2.5 * P.dpr, 5 * P.dpr]); g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(150,118,72,.55)'; g.lineWidth = 0.8 * P.dpr; g.stroke(); g.setLineDash([]); }
  }
  // بيانات المدينة: أزقة v18 وكتل البيوت من CITYV.live (تُحفظ بعد أول تحميل للمدينة)، وإلا الحلقات والشوارع الأصلية
  const RINGS0 = [[72, 7], [96, 4.4], [118, 4.4], [143, 4.4]], THRU0 = [[0, 62, 160, 5], [180, 62, 160, 5], [270, 62, 160, 7], [90, 143, 160, 5], [225, 72, 143, 4.4], [315, 72, 143, 4.4], [45, 118, 143, 4.4], [135, 118, 143, 4.4]];
  function cityData() {
    if (M.city) return M.city;
    const L = CITYV && CITYV.live, v = L && L.v18; if (!v || !v.strips) return null;
    M.city = { strips: v.strips.map(s => ({ pts: s.pts.map(p => [p[0], p[1]]), w: s.w })), units: v.units.map(u => [u.FL, u.FR, u.BR, u.BL].map(p => [p[0], p[1]])), wells: [] };
    bump(); return M.city;
  }
  const CP = (r, deg) => [CITY.x + r * Math.cos(deg * D2R), CITY.z - r * Math.sin(deg * D2R)];
  function stables() { const s = [[CITY.x - 40, CITY.z + 128], [CITY.x + 40, CITY.z + 128]]; let main = 0;
    try { const L = window.V19 && V19.H && V19.H.list; if (L && L.length) { const h = L.find(m => !m.owned) || L[0]; if (Math.hypot(h.x - s[1][0], h.z - s[1][1]) < Math.hypot(h.x - s[0][0], h.z - s[0][1])) main = 1; } } catch (e) {}
    return { s, main }; }
  function landmarks() {
    const HL = WORLD.hill, st = stables();
    return [
      { k: 'palace', x: HL.x, z: HL.z, n: 'القصر', big: 1 },
      { k: 'market', x: CITY.x, z: CITY.z + 18, n: 'السوق', big: 1 },
      { k: 'plaza', x: CITY.x - 30, z: CITY.z - 6, n: 'الساحة' },
      { k: 'mosque', x: CP(92.5, 165)[0], z: CP(92.5, 165)[1], n: 'الجامع' },
      { k: 'barracks', x: CP(132, 10)[0], z: CP(132, 10)[1], n: 'الثكنة' }, { k: 'barracks', x: CP(132, 170)[0], z: CP(132, 170)[1], n: 'الثكنة' },
      { k: 'fort', x: CP(132, 260)[0], z: CP(132, 260)[1], n: 'الحصن' }, { k: 'fort', x: CP(132, 280)[0], z: CP(132, 280)[1], n: 'الحصن' },
      { k: 'stable', x: st.s[st.main][0], z: st.s[st.main][1], n: 'إسطبل الخيل', main: 1 }, { k: 'stable', x: st.s[1 - st.main][0], z: st.s[1 - st.main][1], n: 'الإسطبل' },
    ];
  }
  function circ(g, P, x, z, r) { g.beginPath(); g.arc(P.X(x), P.Y(z), Math.max(0.5, r * P.s), 0, TAU); }
  function cityIcon(g, x, y, r, dpr) {
    g.fillStyle = '#ead9b2'; g.strokeStyle = '#4e3d2a'; g.lineWidth = Math.max(2, r * 0.22); g.beginPath(); g.arc(x, y, r, 0, TAU); g.fill(); g.stroke();
    g.strokeStyle = GOLD; g.lineWidth = Math.max(1, r * 0.08); g.beginPath(); g.arc(x, y, r * 0.78, 0, TAU); g.stroke();
    for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) { const a = (k + 0.5) / 8 * TAU; g.fillStyle = '#4e3d2a'; g.beginPath(); g.arc(x + Math.cos(a) * r, y + Math.sin(a) * r, Math.max(1.4, r * 0.2), 0, TAU); g.fill(); }
    domeIcon(g, x, y + r * 0.15, r * 0.55);
  }
  function domeIcon(g, x, y, k) {
    g.fillStyle = GOLD_L; g.strokeStyle = '#4a3214'; g.lineWidth = Math.max(1, k * 0.14);
    g.beginPath(); g.rect(x - k * 0.8, y - k * 0.1, k * 1.6, k * 0.75); g.fill(); g.stroke();
    g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(x - k * 0.62, y - k * 0.1); g.quadraticCurveTo(x - k * 0.62, y - k * 0.95, x, y - k * 1.15); g.quadraticCurveTo(x + k * 0.62, y - k * 0.95, x + k * 0.62, y - k * 0.1); g.closePath(); g.fill(); g.stroke();
  }
  function horseHead(g, x, y, k, fill, stroke) {
    g.save(); g.translate(x, y); g.scale(k, k);
    g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(-0.5, 0.95); g.lineTo(-0.42, 0.18); g.quadraticCurveTo(-0.6, -0.36, -0.2, -0.7); g.lineTo(-0.14, -1.0); g.lineTo(0.04, -0.74);
    g.quadraticCurveTo(0.46, -0.62, 0.82, 0.04); g.quadraticCurveTo(0.96, 0.33, 0.72, 0.4); g.lineTo(0.3, 0.24); g.quadraticCurveTo(0.12, 0.52, 0.16, 0.95); g.closePath();
    g.fillStyle = fill; g.fill(); g.lineWidth = 0.12; g.strokeStyle = stroke; g.lineJoin = 'round'; g.stroke();
    g.fillStyle = stroke; g.beginPath(); g.arc(0.18, -0.38, 0.08, 0, TAU); g.fill(); g.restore();
  }
  function badge(g, x, y, r, dpr, ring, bg) { g.beginPath(); g.arc(x, y, r, 0, TAU); g.fillStyle = bg || NAVY; g.fill(); g.lineWidth = Math.max(1.2, r * 0.18); g.strokeStyle = ring || GOLD_L; g.stroke(); }
  function lmIcon(g, k, x, y, u) {
    g.lineJoin = 'round';
    if (k === 'mosque') { domeIcon(g, x - u * 0.2, y + u * 0.2, u * 0.8); g.fillStyle = GOLD_L; g.strokeStyle = '#4a3214'; g.lineWidth = Math.max(1, u * 0.1); g.beginPath(); g.rect(x + u * 0.55, y - u * 0.9, u * 0.24, u * 1.25); g.fill(); g.stroke();
      g.beginPath(); g.arc(x + u * 0.67, y - u * 1.08, u * 0.16, 0, TAU); g.fill(); g.stroke(); }
    else if (k === 'palace') { g.fillStyle = GOLD_L; g.strokeStyle = '#4a3214'; g.lineWidth = Math.max(1, u * 0.1); g.beginPath(); g.rect(x - u, y - u * 0.25, u * 2, u * 0.85); g.fill(); g.stroke(); for (const s of [-1, 1]) { g.beginPath(); g.rect(x + s * u - u * 0.25, y - u * 0.75, u * 0.5, u * 1.35); g.fill(); g.stroke(); } domeIcon(g, x, y - u * 0.2, u * 0.55); }
    else if (k === 'fort' || k === 'barracks') { g.fillStyle = k === 'fort' ? '#cdb58a' : '#d9c39a'; g.strokeStyle = '#4a3214'; g.lineWidth = Math.max(1, u * 0.1); g.beginPath();
      g.moveTo(x - u * 0.7, y + u * 0.6); g.lineTo(x - u * 0.7, y - u * 0.5); for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) { const xa = x - u * 0.7 + i * u * 0.35; g.lineTo(xa, y - u * 0.75); g.lineTo(xa + u * 0.175, y - u * 0.75); g.lineTo(xa + u * 0.175, y - u * 0.5); g.lineTo(xa + u * 0.35, y - u * 0.5); }
      g.lineTo(x + u * 0.7, y + u * 0.6); g.closePath(); g.fill(); g.stroke(); if (k === 'barracks') { g.fillStyle = '#8b2a22'; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(x, y - u * 0.75); g.lineTo(x, y - u * 1.45); g.lineTo(x + u * 0.5, y - u * 1.27); g.lineTo(x, y - u * 1.1); g.fill(); } }
    else if (k === 'inn') { g.fillStyle = '#efe2c4'; g.strokeStyle = '#4a3214'; g.lineWidth = Math.max(1, u * 0.1); g.beginPath(); g.rect(x - u * 0.75, y - u * 0.3, u * 1.5, u * 0.9); g.fill(); g.stroke(); g.fillStyle = '#b05a3a'; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(x - u * 0.9, y - u * 0.3); g.lineTo(x, y - u * 0.95); g.lineTo(x + u * 0.9, y - u * 0.3); g.closePath(); g.fill(); g.stroke();
      g.fillStyle = '#4a3214'; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(x - u * 0.2, y + u * 0.6); g.lineTo(x - u * 0.2, y + u * 0.15); g.arc(x, y + u * 0.15, u * 0.2, Math.PI, 0); g.lineTo(x + u * 0.2, y + u * 0.6); g.fill(); }
    else if (k === 'stable') { badge(g, x, y, u * 0.95, 1, GOLD_L, '#5a3a1c'); horseHead(g, x, y + u * 0.05, u * 0.62, '#f3dfa8', '#2a1a0a'); }
  }
  function drawCity(g, P, labels) {
    const cx = CITY.x, cz = CITY.z, R = CITY_WR, pr = R * P.s, X0 = P.X(cx), Y0 = P.Y(cz), dpr = P.dpr;
    if (X0 + pr + 60 * dpr < 0 || Y0 + pr + 60 * dpr < 0 || X0 - pr - 60 * dpr > P.W || Y0 - pr - 60 * dpr > P.H) return;
    if (pr < 18 * dpr) { const ir = Math.max(pr, 8 * dpr); cityIcon(g, X0, Y0, ir, dpr); if (labels) labels.push({ t: CITYV.name || 'مدينة الياسمين', x: cx, z: cz, dy: -(ir / dpr) - 10, size: 12.5, pri: 0, col: '#6a3c0c', w: 700 }); return; }
    const cd = cityData(), HL = WORLD.hill;
    circ(g, P, cx, cz, R + 4); g.fillStyle = '#e3d1a8'; g.fill();
    circ(g, P, cx, cz, 64); g.fillStyle = '#efe3c3'; g.fill();
    circ(g, P, HL.x, HL.z, HL.r2 || 60); g.fillStyle = '#d7c193'; g.fill();
    g.fillStyle = '#ecd7a6'; g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(120,84,40,.7)'; g.lineWidth = Math.max(1, 0.8 * dpr); g.beginPath(); g.rect(P.X(cx - 50), P.Y(cz - 7), 100 * P.s, 50 * P.s); g.fill(); g.stroke();
    if (P.s > 1.4) { const cols = ['#b6452f', '#d79a32', '#2f5f73', '#7a8c3a', '#9a5a2c']; let n = 0;
      for (let rz = cz - 2; rz < cz + 40; rz += 9) for (let rx = cx - 44; rx < cx + 44; rx += 8) { g.fillStyle = cols[(n++ * 7) % 5]; g.fillRect(P.X(rx), P.Y(rz), 5 * P.s, 3.2 * P.s); } }
    g.lineJoin = 'round'; g.lineCap = 'round';
    const strips = cd ? cd.strips : null;
    if (strips) {
      for (const s of strips) { poly(g, P, s.pts); g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(110,80,46,.75)'; g.lineWidth = Math.max(s.w * P.s + 1.4 * dpr, 1.6 * dpr); g.stroke(); }
      for (const s of strips) { poly(g, P, s.pts); g.strokeStyle = '#f7eed6'; g.lineWidth = Math.max(s.w * P.s, 0.9 * dpr); g.stroke(); }
    } else {
      for (const [r, w] of RINGS0) { circ(g, P, cx, cz, r); g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(110,80,46,.75)'; g.lineWidth = Math.max(w * P.s + 1.4 * dpr, 1.6 * dpr); g.stroke(); g.strokeStyle = '#f7eed6'; g.lineWidth = Math.max(w * P.s, 0.9 * dpr); g.stroke(); }
      for (const [a, r0, r1, w] of THRU0) { const p0 = CP(r0, a), p1 = CP(r1, a); g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(P.X(p0[0]), P.Y(p0[1])); g.lineTo(P.X(p1[0]), P.Y(p1[1])); g.strokeStyle = '#f7eed6'; g.lineWidth = Math.max(w * P.s, 0.9 * dpr); g.stroke(); }
    }
    if (cd && P.s > 0.7) { g.fillStyle = 'rgba(196,146,104,.95)'; g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(84,52,28,.6)'; g.lineWidth = Math.max(0.6, 0.6 * dpr);
      for (const u of cd.units) { poly(g, P, u); g.closePath(); g.fill(); if (P.s > 1.2) g.stroke(); } }
    circ(g, P, HL.x, HL.z, 36); g.strokeStyle = GOLD; g.lineWidth = Math.max(1.4 * dpr, 2 * P.s); g.stroke();
    // السور والأبراج والبوابات
    circ(g, P, cx, cz, R); g.strokeStyle = '#4b3b29'; g.lineWidth = Math.max(4.2 * P.s, 2.6 * dpr); g.stroke();
    g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(230,190,104,.9)'; g.lineWidth = Math.max(1.2 * P.s, 0.9 * dpr); g.stroke();
    for (let k = 0; k < 24; k++) { const a = (k + 0.5) * 15; if (Math.min(...[0, 90, 180, 270, 360].map(q => Math.abs(a - q))) < 9) continue; const p = CP(R, a);
      g.fillStyle = '#4b3b29'; g.beginPath(); g.arc(P.X(p[0]), P.Y(p[1]), Math.max(3.2 * P.s, 1.8 * dpr), 0, TAU); g.fill(); }
    const GN = { 0: 'باب الشرق', 90: 'باب الشمال', 180: 'باب الغرب', 270: 'باب الجنوب' };
    for (const a of [0, 90, 180, 270]) { const p = CP(R, a), gx = P.X(p[0]), gy = P.Y(p[1]), u = Math.max(5 * dpr, 7 * P.s);
      g.save(); g.translate(gx, gy); g.rotate(-a * D2R + Math.PI / 2); g.fillStyle = GOLD_L; g.strokeStyle = '#3a2a14'; g.lineWidth = Math.max(1, 0.9 * dpr);
      g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(-u, u * 0.55); g.lineTo(-u, -u * 0.1); g.quadraticCurveTo(-u, -u, 0, -u * 1.05); g.quadraticCurveTo(u, -u, u, -u * 0.1); g.lineTo(u, u * 0.55); g.closePath(); g.fill(); g.stroke();
      g.fillStyle = '#3a2a14'; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(-u * 0.42, u * 0.55); g.lineTo(-u * 0.42, 0); g.quadraticCurveTo(0, -u * 0.62, u * 0.42, 0); g.lineTo(u * 0.42, u * 0.55); g.closePath(); g.fill(); g.restore();
      if (labels && pr > 120 * dpr) labels.push({ t: GN[a], x: p[0] + Math.cos(a * D2R) * 22, z: p[1] - Math.sin(a * D2R) * 22, size: 9, pri: 4, col: BROWN }); }
    // المعالم
    const u = cl(7 * P.s, 4.5 * dpr, 10 * dpr);
    for (const L of landmarks()) {
      if (L.k === 'market' || L.k === 'plaza') { if (labels && pr > 70 * dpr) labels.push({ t: L.n, x: L.x, z: L.z, size: L.big ? 11 : 9.5, pri: 2, col: BROWN, at: 0 }); continue; }
      if (pr < 55 * dpr && !(L.k === 'stable' && L.main)) continue;
      const iu = L.k === 'palace' ? u * 1.25 : u, ix = P.X(L.x), iy = P.Y(L.z); lmIcon(g, L.k, ix, iy, iu); (P.placed || (P.placed = [])).push({ x0: ix - iu, y0: iy - iu * 1.2, x1: ix + iu, y1: iy + iu });
      if (labels && (pr > 95 * dpr || (L.main && pr > 55 * dpr))) labels.push({ t: L.n, x: L.x, z: L.z, dy: u * 1.6 / dpr, size: L.big ? 11 : 9.5, pri: L.big || L.main ? 2 : 3, col: BROWN });
    }
    if (labels) labels.push({ t: CITYV.name || 'مدينة الياسمين', x: cx, z: cz, dy: -(pr / dpr) - 12, size: 13, pri: 0, col: '#6a3c0c', w: 700 });
  }
  function vilIcon(g, x, y, k) {
    const hs = [[-0.95, 0.25, 0.8], [0.9, 0.35, 0.75], [0, -0.45, 0.95]];
    for (const [ox, oy, sc] of hs) { const s = k * sc, hx = x + ox * k, hy = y + oy * k;
      g.fillStyle = '#f2e4c4'; g.strokeStyle = INK; g.lineWidth = Math.max(0.9, k * 0.13); g.beginPath(); g.rect(hx - s * 0.6, hy - s * 0.15, s * 1.2, s * 0.85); g.fill(); g.stroke();
      g.fillStyle = '#b4583a'; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(hx - s * 0.78, hy - s * 0.12); g.lineTo(hx, hy - s * 0.85); g.lineTo(hx + s * 0.78, hy - s * 0.12); g.closePath(); g.fill(); g.stroke(); }
  }
  function drawVillages(g, P, labels) {
    const dpr = P.dpr;
    for (const v of VILLAGES) {
      if (v.city) continue; const x = P.X(v.x), y = P.Y(v.z), R = (v.R || 64) * P.s;
      if (x < -R - 80 * dpr || y < -R - 40 * dpr || x > P.W + R + 80 * dpr || y > P.H + R + 40 * dpr) continue;
      if (R > 9 * dpr) { g.beginPath(); g.arc(x, y, R, 0, TAU); g.fillStyle = 'rgba(236,222,186,.8)'; g.fill(); g.setLineDash([3 * dpr, 2.5 * dpr]); g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(110,78,40,.8)'; g.lineWidth = Math.max(1, 1.1 * dpr); g.stroke(); g.setLineDash([]); }
      const k = cl(R * 0.32, 3.6 * dpr, 9 * dpr); vilIcon(g, x, y, k); (P.placed || (P.placed = [])).push({ x0: x - k * 1.6, y0: y - k * 1.4, x1: x + k * 1.6, y1: y + k });
      if (labels) labels.push({ t: v.name, x: v.x, z: v.z, dy: (Math.max(R, k * 1.4) / dpr) + 9, size: 10.5, pri: 1, col: INK, w: 700 });
    }
  }
  // الأسماء: من اليمين لليسار، بهالة ورقية، مع تجنّب التداخل (الأهم أولاً)
  function placeLabels(g, P, labels) {
    const placed = P.placed || (P.placed = []), dpr = P.dpr;
    labels.sort((a, b) => a.pri - b.pri);
    g.textAlign = 'center'; g.textBaseline = 'middle'; try { g.direction = 'rtl'; } catch (e) {}
    for (const L of labels) {
      const fs = Math.round(L.size * dpr * (P.lab || 1)); g.font = (L.w || 600) + ' ' + fs + 'px ' + FONT;
      const w = g.measureText(L.t).width, h = fs * 1.15, bx = P.X(L.x), by = P.Y(L.z) + (L.dy || 0) * dpr;
      const cands = L.at === 0 ? [[0, 0]] : [[0, 0], [0, -1.6], [0, 1.6], [1, 0], [-1, 0], [0, -3], [0, 3]];
      for (const [ux, uy] of cands) {
        const x = bx + ux * (w * 0.55 + 6 * dpr), y = by + uy * h;
        const r = { x0: x - w / 2 - 2 * dpr, y0: y - h / 2, x1: x + w / 2 + 2 * dpr, y1: y + h / 2 };
        if (P.clipLab && (r.x0 < 2 || r.y0 < 2 || r.x1 > P.W - 2 || r.y1 > P.H - 2)) continue;
        let hit = false; for (const q of placed) if (r.x0 < q.x1 && q.x0 < r.x1 && r.y0 < q.y1 && q.y0 < r.y1) { hit = true; break; }
        if (hit) continue; placed.push(r);
        if (L.region) { g.globalAlpha = 0.62; g.fillStyle = L.col; g.lineWidth = 2.4 * dpr; g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(246,236,210,.55)'; g.strokeText(L.t, x, y); g.fillText(L.t, x, y); g.globalAlpha = 1; }
        else { g.lineJoin = 'round'; g.lineWidth = 3.2 * dpr; g.strokeStyle = L.halo || HALO; g.strokeText(L.t, x, y); g.fillStyle = L.col || INK; g.fillText(L.t, x, y); }
        break;
      }
    }
  }
  const NJ = 'ءآأؤإاةدذرزوى';
  function kash(s) { return s.split(' ').map(w => { if (w.length < 4) return w; for (let k = Math.floor(w.length / 2); k > 0; k--) if (!NJ.includes(w[k]) && k < w.length - 1) return w.slice(0, k + 1) + 'ــ' + w.slice(k + 1); return w; }).join('  '); }

  /* ── (٤) تركيب منظر كامل في لوحة: P = { W, H, dpr, s (بكسل/م), X(x), Y(z), x0..z1, mini } ── */
  function drawWorld(g, P, treeList) {
    drawTerrain(g, P);
    try { drawRoads(g, P); } catch (e) { warn('roads', e); }
    if (P.s > 0.12 && treeList) try { drawTrees(g, P, treeList); } catch (e) { warn('trees', e); }
    const labels = [];
    try { drawVillages(g, P, labels); } catch (e) { warn('villages', e); }
    try { drawCity(g, P, labels); } catch (e) { warn('city', e); }
    if (!P.mini && M.reg) { const fit = P.s / (P.s0 || P.s); if (fit < 5) for (const r of M.reg) labels.push({ t: kash(r.name), x: r.x, z: r.z, size: cl(12 + Math.sqrt(r.area) * 0.32, 13, 21), pri: fit < 2 ? 0.5 : 5, col: '#3c2a16', region: 1, w: 700, at: 1 });
      if (fit < 3) for (const q of M.seaLab) labels.push({ t: kash('البحر'), x: q.x, z: q.z, size: 17, pri: 6, col: '#d8e6ea', region: 1, w: 700 }); }
    try { placeLabels(g, P, labels); } catch (e) { warn('labels', e); }
  }
  function view(W, H, dpr, cx, cz, s, mini) {
    const hw = W / 2 / s, hh = H / 2 / s;
    return { W, H, dpr, s, mini, cx, cz, x0: cx - hw, x1: cx + hw, z0: cz - hh, z1: cz + hh, X: x => (x - cx) * s + W / 2, Y: z => (z - cz) * s + H / 2 };
  }

  /* ── (٥) الخريطة المصغّرة: لوحة جاهزة حول اللاعب لكل مستوى تكبير، تُبنى على مراحل وتُنسخ كل ١٠٠م.ث مع العلامات ── */
  const MB = { tile: null, bx: 0, bz: 0, H: 0, zi: -1, cw: 0, ver: 0, gen: null, ovl: null, ovW: 0 };
  function* mmGen(cw, zi, bx, bz) {
    const R = MMP_ZOOM[zi], H = R * 1.35, s = cw / (2 * R), N = Math.ceil(2 * H * s), dpr = MMP.dpr || 1, ver = M.ver;
    const cv = document.createElement('canvas'); cv.width = cv.height = N; const g = cv.getContext('2d');
    const P = view(N, N, dpr, bx, bz, s, true); P.clipLab = false; P.lab = 0.95;
    if (!(M.L0 && M.L0.ready)) { const q = 64, J = terrNew(bx - H, bz - H, 2 * H / q, q, q, 2.4, 0); terrRun(J, Infinity); P.quick = { cv: toCanvas(J), x0: bx - H, z0: bz - H, w: 2 * H }; }
    yield;
    let trees = null;
    if (zi <= 1) { trees = treesExact(bx - H, bz - H, bx + H, bz + H); yield; }
    else if (M.TD) trees = treesIndexed(P);
    drawWorld(g, P, trees);
    return { tile: cv, bx, bz, H, zi, cw, ver };
  }
  function mmEnsure(tEnd) {
    const cv = $('mmC'); if (!cv || !cv.width) return;
    const cw = cv.width, zi = MMP.zi, H = MMP_ZOOM[zi] * 1.35;
    const need = !MB.tile || MB.zi !== zi || MB.cw !== cw || Math.hypot(PL.x - MB.bx, PL.z - MB.bz) > H * 0.22 || (MB.ver !== M.ver && !MB.gen);
    if (need && !MB.gen) MB.gen = mmGen(cw, zi, PL.x, PL.z);
    if (MB.gen) {
      const sync = !MB.tile || MB.zi !== zi || MB.cw !== cw;     // أول بناء أو تغيّر التكبير: يُكمل فوراً
      for (;;) { const r = MB.gen.next(); if (r.done) { Object.assign(MB, r.value); MB.gen = null; break; } if (!sync && now() > tEnd) break; }
    }
  }
  function mmOverlay(W, dpr) {
    if (MB.ovl && MB.ovW === W) return MB.ovl;
    const cv = document.createElement('canvas'); cv.width = cv.height = W; const g = cv.getContext('2d'), c = W / 2;
    const gr = g.createRadialGradient(c, c, c * 0.62, c, c, c); gr.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(14,27,51,0)'); gr.addColorStop(0.85, 'rgba(14,27,51,.18)'); gr.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(14,27,51,.62)');
    g.fillStyle = gr; g.beginPath(); g.arc(c, c, c, 0, TAU); g.fill();
    g.beginPath(); g.arc(c, c, c - 1.2 * dpr, 0, TAU); g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(240,207,120,.85)'; g.lineWidth = 1.6 * dpr; g.stroke();
    g.font = '700 ' + Math.round(11 * dpr) + 'px ' + FONT; g.textAlign = 'center'; g.textBaseline = 'middle';
    g.lineWidth = 3 * dpr; g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(14,27,51,.9)'; g.strokeText('ش', c, 12 * dpr); g.fillStyle = GOLD_L; g.fillText('ش', c, 12 * dpr);
    MB.ovl = cv; MB.ovW = W; return cv;
  }
  function ownedHorse() { try { const L = window.V19 && V19.H && V19.H.list; return L ? L.find(m => m.owned) || null : null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function playerArrow(g, x, y, k, dpr) {
    const ang = Math.atan2(-Math.cos(PL.face), -Math.sin(PL.face));
    g.beginPath(); g.arc(x, y, 11 * k * dpr, 0, TAU); g.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,248,226,.28)'; g.fill();
    g.save(); g.translate(x, y); g.rotate(ang); g.lineJoin = 'round';
    g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(12 * k * dpr, 0); g.lineTo(-7 * k * dpr, 7.5 * k * dpr); g.lineTo(-3 * k * dpr, 0); g.lineTo(-7 * k * dpr, -7.5 * k * dpr); g.closePath();
    g.fillStyle = GOLD_L; g.strokeStyle = NAVY; g.lineWidth = 2 * dpr; g.fill(); g.stroke();
    g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(9 * k * dpr, 0); g.lineTo(-3 * k * dpr, 0); g.lineTo(-5 * k * dpr, -5 * k * dpr); g.closePath(); g.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.55)'; g.fill(); g.restore();
  }
  function horseMark(g, x, y, k, dpr) { badge(g, x, y, 8.5 * k * dpr, dpr, GOLD_L, '#6b2f1a'); horseHead(g, x, y + 0.3 * dpr, 5.6 * k * dpr, '#f6e3ad', '#2a1408'); }
  function mmDraw() {
    const cv = $('mmC'); if (!cv || !cv.width || !MB.tile) return;
    const g = cv.getContext('2d'), W = cv.width, c = W / 2, dpr = MMP.dpr || 1, R = MMP_ZOOM[MB.zi], s = W / (2 * R);
    g.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0); g.clearRect(0, 0, W, W);
    g.save(); g.beginPath(); g.arc(c, c, c, 0, TAU); g.clip();
    g.fillStyle = SEA_FILL; g.fillRect(0, 0, W, W);
    g.drawImage(MB.tile, c + (MB.bx - MB.H - PL.x) * s, c + (MB.bz - MB.H - PL.z) * s);
    const h = ownedHorse();
    if (h && !h.mounted) { const dx = (h.x - PL.x) * s, dz = (h.z - PL.z) * s, d = Math.hypot(dx, dz), lim = c - 12 * dpr;
      if (d < lim) horseMark(g, c + dx, c + dz, 0.85, dpr); else { const k = lim / d; horseMark(g, c + dx * k, c + dz * k, 0.7, dpr); } }
    g.drawImage(mmOverlay(W, dpr), 0, 0);
    playerArrow(g, c, c, 0.95, dpr);
    g.restore();
  }

  /* ── (٦) خريطة العالم: سحب وتكبير بالإصبعين/العجلة/الأزرار؛ المنظر يُرسم عند الثبات، وأثناء الحركة تُحرَّك النسخة الجاهزة ── */
  const WV = { cx: 0, cz: 0, s: 0, s0: 0, smax: 5, init: false, cv: null, R: null, dirty: true, lastG: 0, ptr: new Map(), pin: null, tap: null, comp: null, compD: 0, ui: false, renderVer: -1 };
  function fitView(W, H) { const t = M.L0 ? M.L0.b.tight : { x0: -CFG.HW, x1: CFG.HW, z0: -CFG.HH, z1: CFG.HH }; WV.s0 = Math.min(W / (t.x1 - t.x0), H / (t.z1 - t.z0)) * 0.96; WV.cx = (t.x0 + t.x1) / 2; WV.cz = (t.z0 + t.z1) / 2; WV.s = WV.s0; }
  function clampView() {
    WV.s = cl(WV.s, WV.s0 * 0.85, WV.smax); const t = M.L0 ? M.L0.b.tight : { x0: -CFG.HW, x1: CFG.HW, z0: -CFG.HH, z1: CFG.HH };
    WV.cx = cl(WV.cx, t.x0, t.x1); WV.cz = cl(WV.cz, t.z0, t.z1); WV.dirty = true; WV.lastG = now();
  }
  function wmLayout() {
    const cv = $('wC'); if (!cv) return; wmUI();
    const dpr = WM.dpr = Math.min(devicePixelRatio || 1, 2), W = Math.round(innerWidth * dpr), H = Math.round(innerHeight * dpr);
    if (cv.width !== W || cv.height !== H) { cv.width = W; cv.height = H; }
    WV.cv = WV.cv || document.createElement('canvas'); WV.cv.width = W; WV.cv.height = H; WM.W = W; WM.H = H;
    const sm = WV.s0; fitView(innerWidth, innerHeight); if (WV.init && sm) { WV.s0 = Math.min(WV.s0, WV.s0); } 
    if (!WV.init) { WV.init = true; } else if (WV.last) { WV.cx = WV.last.cx; WV.cz = WV.last.cz; WV.s = WV.last.s; }
    WV.smax = Math.max(4, WV.s0 * 8); clampView(); wmRender(); wmDraw();
  }
  function wmRender() {
    if (!WV.cv) return; const dpr = WM.dpr || 1, W = WV.cv.width, H = WV.cv.height, g = WV.cv.getContext('2d');
    const P = view(W, H, dpr, WV.cx, WV.cz, WV.s * dpr, false); P.s0 = WV.s0 * dpr; P.clipLab = true;
    g.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
    let trees = [];
    if (P.s > 0.16 * dpr) { const cells = ((P.x1 - P.x0) / 64 + 1) * ((P.z1 - P.z0) / 64 + 1); trees = cells < 140 ? treesExact(P.x0, P.z0, P.x1, P.z1) : treesIndexed(P).filter(t => t.src !== 2 || P.s < 0.5 * dpr); }
    drawWorld(g, P, trees);
    WV.R = { cx: WV.cx, cz: WV.cz, s: WV.s }; WV.renderVer = M.ver; WV.dirty = true;
  }
  function compass(dpr) {
    if (WV.comp && WV.compD === dpr) return WV.comp;
    const r = 30 * dpr, cv = document.createElement('canvas'); cv.width = cv.height = Math.ceil(r * 2 + 26 * dpr); const g = cv.getContext('2d'), c = cv.width / 2;
    g.beginPath(); g.arc(c, c, r * 0.62, 0, TAU); g.fillStyle = 'rgba(246,236,210,.55)'; g.fill(); g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(90,60,24,.8)'; g.lineWidth = 1.2 * dpr; g.stroke();
    g.beginPath(); g.arc(c, c, r * 0.5, 0, TAU); g.setLineDash([2 * dpr, 2 * dpr]); g.stroke(); g.setLineDash([]);
    for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) {                         // نجمة ثمانية الرؤوس: الرؤوس الأربعة الكبرى والأربعة الصغرى، كل رأس معيّن بوجهين (فاتح/داكن)
      const a = k * Math.PI / 4 - Math.PI / 2, L = (k % 2 ? 0.56 : 1) * r, wdt = (k % 2 ? 0.13 : 0.2) * r, ca = Math.cos(a), sa = Math.sin(a), px = -sa, py = ca;
      g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(c, c); g.lineTo(c + ca * L, c + sa * L); g.lineTo(c + ca * wdt * 1.2 + px * wdt, c + sa * wdt * 1.2 + py * wdt); g.closePath(); g.fillStyle = k % 2 ? '#e9cf8a' : GOLD_L; g.fill();
      g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(c, c); g.lineTo(c + ca * L, c + sa * L); g.lineTo(c + ca * wdt * 1.2 - px * wdt, c + sa * wdt * 1.2 - py * wdt); g.closePath(); g.fillStyle = k % 2 ? '#8a6524' : '#1b2e52'; g.fill();
      g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(c + ca * L, c + sa * L); g.lineTo(c + ca * wdt * 1.2 + px * wdt, c + sa * wdt * 1.2 + py * wdt); g.lineTo(c, c); g.lineTo(c + ca * wdt * 1.2 - px * wdt, c + sa * wdt * 1.2 - py * wdt); g.closePath(); g.strokeStyle = '#2a1c0c'; g.lineWidth = 0.9 * dpr; g.stroke();
    }
    g.beginPath(); g.arc(c, c, 2.6 * dpr, 0, TAU); g.fillStyle = '#2a1c0c'; g.fill();
    g.font = '700 ' + Math.round(13 * dpr) + 'px ' + FONT; g.textAlign = 'center'; g.textBaseline = 'middle'; g.lineWidth = 3 * dpr; g.strokeStyle = HALO;
    g.strokeText('ش', c, c - r - 6 * dpr); g.fillStyle = '#7a1e12'; g.fillText('ش', c, c - r - 6 * dpr);
    WV.comp = cv; WV.compD = dpr; return cv;
  }
  function scaleBar(g, W, H, dpr) {
    const s = WV.s * dpr, nice = [25, 50, 100, 250, 500, 1000, 2000]; let m = nice[0]; for (const q of nice) if (q * s <= 110 * dpr) m = q;
    const L = m * s, x1 = W - 16 * dpr, x0 = x1 - L, y = H - 20 * dpr;
    g.fillStyle = 'rgba(246,236,210,.75)'; g.fillRect(x0 - 6 * dpr, y - 16 * dpr, L + 12 * dpr, 26 * dpr);
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) { g.fillStyle = i % 2 ? '#f6ecd2' : NAVY; g.fillRect(x0 + i * L / 4, y, L / 4, 4 * dpr); }
    g.strokeStyle = NAVY; g.lineWidth = 1 * dpr; g.strokeRect(x0, y, L, 4 * dpr);
    g.font = '700 ' + Math.round(10 * dpr) + 'px ' + FONT; g.textAlign = 'center'; g.textBaseline = 'middle'; try { g.direction = 'rtl'; } catch (e) {}
    g.fillStyle = NAVY; g.fillText(m >= 1000 ? arD(m / 1000) + ' كم' : arD(m) + ' م', (x0 + x1) / 2, y - 7 * dpr);
  }
  function wmDraw() {
    const cv = $('wC'); if (!cv || !WV.cv || !WV.R) return; const g = cv.getContext('2d'), W = cv.width, H = cv.height, dpr = WM.dpr || 1, R = WV.R, k = WV.s / R.s;
    g.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0); g.fillStyle = SEA_FILL; g.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
    g.imageSmoothingEnabled = true; g.drawImage(WV.cv, W / 2 + (R.cx - WV.cx) * WV.s * dpr - W / 2 * k, H / 2 + (R.cz - WV.cz) * WV.s * dpr - H / 2 * k, W * k, H * k);
    const X = x => (x - WV.cx) * WV.s * dpr + W / 2, Y = z => (z - WV.cz) * WV.s * dpr + H / 2, h = ownedHorse();
    if (h && !h.mounted) { const hx = X(h.x), hy = Y(h.z); horseMark(g, hx, hy, 1.15, dpr);
      if (WV.s > WV.s0 * 2.5) { g.font = '700 ' + Math.round(10 * dpr) + 'px ' + FONT; g.textAlign = 'center'; g.textBaseline = 'middle'; g.lineWidth = 3 * dpr; g.strokeStyle = HALO; g.strokeText('حصانك', hx, hy + 16 * dpr); g.fillStyle = '#6b2f1a'; g.fillText('حصانك', hx, hy + 16 * dpr); } }
    playerArrow(g, X(PL.x), Y(PL.z), 1.15, dpr);
    const cp = compass(dpr); g.drawImage(cp, 10 * dpr, 10 * dpr);
    scaleBar(g, W, H, dpr);
    if (!(M.L0 && M.L0.ready)) { g.font = '700 ' + Math.round(13 * dpr) + 'px ' + FONT; g.textAlign = 'center'; g.fillStyle = GOLD_L; g.fillText('…جارٍ رسم الخريطة', W / 2, H / 2); }
    WV.dirty = false;
  }
  function wmTick(dt) {
    if (!WM.open) return;
    const t = now(), settled = t - WV.lastG > 140 && WV.ptr.size === 0, R = WV.R;
    if (settled && (!R || R.cx !== WV.cx || R.cz !== WV.cz || R.s !== WV.s || (WV.renderVer !== M.ver && t - (WV.rT || 0) > 400))) { wmRender(); WV.rT = now(); }
    WM.t += dt; if (WV.dirty || WM.t > 0.25) { WM.t = 0; wmDraw(); }
  }
  function zoomAt(f, sx, sy) { const b = WV.s, ns = cl(b * f, WV.s0 * 0.85, WV.smax), W = innerWidth, H = innerHeight;
    const wx = WV.cx + (sx - W / 2) / b, wz = WV.cz + (sy - H / 2) / b; WV.s = ns; WV.cx = wx - (sx - W / 2) / ns; WV.cz = wz - (sy - H / 2) / ns; clampView(); }
  function wmUI() {
    if (WV.ui) return; WV.ui = true;
    const st = document.createElement('style'); st.id = 'map20Css';
    st.textContent = '#wmap .wf{left:auto;right:auto;inset-inline-start:auto;left:10px;transform:none;bottom:calc(8px + var(--safeB));max-width:62%;gap:3px 9px;padding:4px 9px;border-radius:10px;background:rgba(246,236,210,.86);border:1px solid #b8913f;color:#1b2a44;font:600 10.5px "Noto Naskh Arabic",Cairo,Tahoma,serif;box-shadow:0 3px 10px rgba(0,0,0,.35)}' +
      '#wmap .wf .lg svg{width:15px;height:15px;flex:none}#wmap #wC{background:#1f3758;cursor:grab}' +
      '#wZ{position:absolute;right:10px;top:calc(58px + var(--safeT));z-index:2;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:7px}' +
      '#wZ button{min-width:58px;height:34px;padding:0 10px;border-radius:10px;border:1px solid #c9a24a;background:linear-gradient(180deg,#1d3157,#0e1b33)!important;color:#f0cf78!important;font:700 12.5px "Noto Naskh Arabic",Cairo,Tahoma,serif;box-shadow:0 3px 10px rgba(0,0,0,.4);touch-action:manipulation;cursor:pointer}' +
      '#wZ button:active{transform:scale(.95)}';
    document.head.appendChild(st);
    const ic = (b) => '<svg viewBox="0 0 20 20">' + b + '</svg>';
    const f = document.querySelector('#wmap .wf');
    if (f) f.innerHTML = [
      [ic('<path d="M3 15h6v-5L6 7 3 10z" fill="#f2e4c4" stroke="#16243d"/><path d="M2.5 10.5L6 6.5l3.5 4" fill="#b4583a" stroke="#16243d"/><path d="M10 16h7v-6l-3.5-3.5L10 10z" fill="#f2e4c4" stroke="#16243d"/><path d="M9.5 10.5L13.5 6l4 4.5" fill="#b4583a" stroke="#16243d"/>'), 'قرية'],
      [ic('<circle cx="10" cy="10" r="7.5" fill="#ead9b2" stroke="#4e3d2a" stroke-width="2"/><path d="M7 12V9.5Q7 6.5 10 6q3 .5 3 3.5V12z" fill="#f0cf78" stroke="#4a3214"/>'), 'المدينة'],
      [ic('<path d="M2 10h16" stroke="#5c4022" stroke-width="5" stroke-linecap="round"/><path d="M2 10h16" stroke="#f4e7c5" stroke-width="2.6" stroke-linecap="round"/>'), 'طريق'],
      [ic('<circle cx="7" cy="9" r="4.6" fill="#7e9550" stroke="#2a3418"/><path d="M14 3q2.4 4 1.7 10.5-1.7 1.3-3.4 0Q11.6 7 14 3z" fill="#3f5a3c" stroke="#1d2c1d"/>'), 'أشجار'],
      [ic('<circle cx="10" cy="10" r="8" fill="#5a3a1c" stroke="#f0cf78" stroke-width="1.6"/><path d="M8 16l.4-5.6Q6.6 6.8 9.4 4.5l.6-2 1.2 2Q14.6 5.6 16 10q.4 1.5-1.2 1.6l-3-1q-1.3 2-1 5.4z" fill="#f3dfa8"/>'), 'إسطبل'],
      [ic('<path d="M17 10L5 16.5 7.8 10 5 3.5z" fill="#f0cf78" stroke="#0e1b33" stroke-width="1.4" stroke-linejoin="round"/>'), 'أنت'],
    ].map(([s, t]) => '<span class="lg">' + s + t + '</span>').join('');
    const z = document.createElement('div'); z.id = 'wZ';
    z.innerHTML = '<button id="wZi" aria-label="تكبير الخريطة">تكبير</button><button id="wZo" aria-label="تصغير الخريطة">تصغير</button><button id="wMe" aria-label="موقعي على الخريطة">موقعي</button>';
    $('wmap').appendChild(z);
    $('wZi').onclick = () => zoomAt(1.8, innerWidth / 2, innerHeight / 2); $('wZo').onclick = () => zoomAt(1 / 1.8, innerWidth / 2, innerHeight / 2);
    $('wMe').onclick = () => { WV.cx = PL.x; WV.cz = PL.z; if (WV.s < WV.s0 * 4) WV.s = WV.s0 * 4; clampView(); };
    const c = $('wC'), pos = e => { const r = c.getBoundingClientRect(); return [e.clientX - r.left, e.clientY - r.top]; };
    c.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => { try { c.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (_) {} const p = pos(e); WV.ptr.set(e.pointerId, p);
      if (WV.ptr.size === 2) { const [a, b] = [...WV.ptr.values()]; WV.pin = { d: Math.hypot(a[0] - b[0], a[1] - b[1]) || 1, s: WV.s, mx: (a[0] + b[0]) / 2, my: (a[1] + b[1]) / 2, cx: WV.cx, cz: WV.cz }; }
      WV.tap = WV.ptr.size === 1 ? { x: p[0], y: p[1], t: now(), mv: 0 } : null; e.preventDefault(); });
    c.addEventListener('pointermove', e => { if (!WV.ptr.has(e.pointerId)) return; const p = pos(e), o = WV.ptr.get(e.pointerId); WV.ptr.set(e.pointerId, p);
      if (WV.ptr.size === 1) { WV.cx -= (p[0] - o[0]) / WV.s; WV.cz -= (p[1] - o[1]) / WV.s; if (WV.tap) WV.tap.mv += Math.hypot(p[0] - o[0], p[1] - o[1]); clampView(); }
      else if (WV.ptr.size === 2 && WV.pin) { const [a, b] = [...WV.ptr.values()], d = Math.hypot(a[0] - b[0], a[1] - b[1]) || 1, mx = (a[0] + b[0]) / 2, my = (a[1] + b[1]) / 2, Q = WV.pin, W = innerWidth, H = innerHeight;
        const wx = Q.cx + (Q.mx - W / 2) / Q.s, wz = Q.cz + (Q.my - H / 2) / Q.s; WV.s = cl(Q.s * d / Q.d, WV.s0 * 0.85, WV.smax); WV.cx = wx - (mx - W / 2) / WV.s; WV.cz = wz - (my - H / 2) / WV.s; clampView(); }
      e.preventDefault(); });
    const up = e => { WV.ptr.delete(e.pointerId); if (WV.ptr.size < 2) WV.pin = null; WV.lastG = now();
      if (WV.tap && WV.ptr.size === 0 && WV.tap.mv < 8 && now() - WV.tap.t < 300) { const t = now(); if (WV.lastTap && t - WV.lastTap.t < 330 && Math.hypot(WV.lastTap.x - WV.tap.x, WV.lastTap.y - WV.tap.y) < 30) { zoomAt(2, WV.tap.x, WV.tap.y); WV.lastTap = null; } else WV.lastTap = { t, x: WV.tap.x, y: WV.tap.y }; }
      if (WV.ptr.size === 0) WV.tap = null; };
    c.addEventListener('pointerup', up); c.addEventListener('pointercancel', up);
    c.addEventListener('wheel', e => { const p = pos(e); zoomAt(Math.exp(-e.deltaY * 0.0016), p[0], p[1]); e.preventDefault(); }, { passive: false });
  }
  function open() {
    if (WM.open || INV.open) return;
    WM.open = true; $('wmap').classList.add('on'); $('wmap').setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');
    document.exitPointerLock?.();
    try { history.pushState({ ow: 'map' }, ''); WM.hist = true; } catch (e) { WM.hist = false; }
    wmLayout();
  }
  function close(fromPop) {
    if (!WM.open) return;
    WM.open = false; WV.last = { cx: WV.cx, cz: WV.cz, s: WV.s }; WV.ptr.clear(); WV.pin = null;
    $('wmap').classList.remove('on'); $('wmap').setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
    const had = WM.hist; WM.hist = false; if (!fromPop && had) { try { history.back(); } catch (e) {} }
  }

  /* ── (٧) منفّذ المهام الخلفية: ميزانية صغيرة أثناء اللعب، أكبر والخريطة مفتوحة ── */
  function work(budget) {
    const tEnd = now() + budget;
    try {
      if (!M.L0) L0Start();
      for (const t of M.want.slice(0, 2)) { if (now() > tEnd) return; const near = Math.abs((t.tx + .5) * TS - PL.x) < TS * 1.2 && Math.abs((t.tz + .5) * TS - PL.z) < TS * 1.2; if (WM.open || near) tileRun(t, tEnd); }
      if (!M.L0.ready) { if (terrRun(M.L0.J, tEnd)) L0Finish(); return; }
      if (!M.TD && typeof TREESET !== 'undefined' && TREESET.ready) tdInit();
      const D = M.TD;
      if (D && !D.old.done && now() < tEnd) { if (tdOld(tEnd)) forestBuild(); }
      if (D && D.old.done && !D.nat.done && WM.open && now() < tEnd) { tdNat(tEnd); if (D.nat.done || now() - D.fT > 700) forestBuild(); }
      tilesTrim();
    } catch (e) { warn('work', e); }
  }
  function mmTick(dt) {
    work(WM.open ? 6 : 3);
    MMP.t += dt; if (MMP.t < 0.1) return; MMP.t = 0;
    try { cityData(); mmEnsure(now() + 4); mmDraw(); } catch (e) { warn('minimap', e); }
  }
  function wmTickAll(dt) { if (WM.open) { work(28); try { wmTick(dt); } catch (e) { warn('wm', e); } } }
  function setRelief(fn) { mapRelief = typeof fn === 'function' ? fn : (x, z) => LIB.height(x, z); M.L0 = null; M.tiles.clear(); M.want.length = 0; MB.tile = null; MB.gen = null; bump(); }
  return { mmTick, mmDraw, mmZoom: d => { MMP.zi = cl(MMP.zi + d, 0, MMP_ZOOM.length - 1); MMP.t = 1; }, mmRebuild: () => { MB.gen = null; MB.tile = null; MMP.t = 1; },
    wmLayout, wmDraw, wmTick: wmTickAll, open, close, setRelief, relief: (x, z) => mapRelief(x, z), work, state: () => ({ L0: !!(M.L0 && M.L0.ready), L0row: M.L0 && M.L0.J ? M.L0.J.j : -1, tiles: M.tiles.size, want: M.want.length, trees: M.TD ? M.TD.n : -1, nat: M.TD ? M.TD.nNat : -1, oldDone: !!(M.TD && M.TD.old.done), natDone: !!(M.TD && M.TD.nat.done), city: !!M.city, reg: M.reg ? M.reg.map(r => r.name) : null, view: { cx: WV.cx, cz: WV.cz, s: WV.s, s0: WV.s0 }, err: M.err, mm: { zi: MB.zi, bx: MB.bx, bz: MB.bz } }), V: WV };
})();

  function aim(bone, child, dirW, w) {
    bone.getWorldPosition(v0); child.getWorldPosition(v1); v1.sub(v0); if (v1.lengthSq() < 1e-8) return; v1.normalize();

  }
  function pose(M, dt) {

  function initUI() {

  }
  try { initUI(); } catch (e) { console.warn('[v20] btn20 ui', e); }
  let tPrev = performance.now();
  (function loop() { requestAnimationFrame(loop); const t = performance.now(); B.dt = Math.min(0.05, (t - tPrev) / 1000); tPrev = t; try { sync(); } catch (e) { if (!B.err) { B.err = 1; console.warn('[v20] btn20', e); } } })();
  recalcDmg(); sync();
  const api = {
    blockDmg, mode, isSpear, B, sync, equip: k => equipItem(k),
    q(down) { if (!hasShield() || MNT.on) return false; B.held.key = !!down; sync(); return true; },          // Q: صد (وإلا يبقى تبديل الكتف)

/* ═══ v20 SHOP20 (task e82a0d36): محل المؤن ومحل الأدوية الجديدان + بائع المؤن v3 وبائعة الأدوية v3 ═══
